        Idea:
Implement the evolved ArtAgents custom node architecture discussed with the user. Treat every executable ArtAgents pipeline stage as a built-in node with typed input/output metadata, while supporting curated installable/wrapped external nodes such as Moirae. Add a node registry, schema, runner/adapter layer, and CLI exposure through the main pipeline tool so users can list, inspect, validate, and run nodes. Preserve legacy pipeline behavior. Use Moirae (https://github.com/peteromallet/Moirae) as the external wrapped-node boundary test in planning and, if feasible, include a local curated node manifest or fixture for it. Design should support optional inputs, output modes including mutate/create_or_replace, cache policy, conditions/graphs, and subprocess isolation. Implement a pragmatic MVP with tests that prove built-in node discovery and at least one node execution/validation path work without requiring expensive APIs or external binaries. Avoid overwriting unrelated dirty worktree changes.

User notes and answers:
- Steer implementation toward the simplest practical MVP. Avoid unnecessary abstraction, broad graph machinery, or premature marketplace/install complexity. Prefer a small node schema, registry, CLI list/inspect/validate/run, wrappers around existing scripts, and focused tests. Preserve legacy behavior. Use GPT-5.5 high instead of GPT-5.4 where possible for this orchestration.
- User steering update: execute the simplest practical MVP. Avoid unnecessary abstraction, broad graph machinery, premature marketplace/install complexity, or large rewrites. Prefer small node schema, registry, CLI list/inspect/validate/run, wrappers around existing scripts, and focused tests. Preserve legacy behavior.         Idea:
Implement the evolved ArtAgents custom node architecture discussed with the user. Treat every executable ArtAgents pipeline stage as a built-in node with typed input/output metadata, while supporting curated installable/wrapped external nodes such as Moirae. Add a node registry, schema, runner/adapter layer, and CLI exposure through the main pipeline tool so users can list, inspect, validate, and run nodes. Preserve legacy pipeline behavior. Use Moirae (https://github.com/peteromallet/Moirae) as the external wrapped-node boundary test in planning and, if feasible, include a local curated node manifest or fixture for it. Design should support optional inputs, output modes including mutate/create_or_replace, cache policy, conditions/graphs, and subprocess isolation. Implement a pragmatic MVP with tests that prove built-in node discovery and at least one node execution/validation path work without requiring expensive APIs or external binaries. Avoid overwriting unrelated dirty worktree changes.

User notes and answers:
- Steer implementation toward the simplest practical MVP. Avoid unnecessary abstraction, broad graph machinery, or premature marketplace/install complexity. Prefer a small node schema, registry, CLI list/inspect/validate/run, wrappers around existing scripts, and focused tests. Preserve legacy behavior. Use GPT-5.5 high instead of GPT-5.4 where possible for this orchestration.

Prefer small node schema, registry, CLI list/inspect/validate/run, wrappers around existing scripts, and focused tests. Preserve legacy behavior. NodeDefinition, NodeValidationError, load_node_manifest, validate_node_definition\n+\n+\n+CURATED_PACKAGE = \"artagents.nodes.curated\"\n+\n+\n+class NodeRegistryError(NodeValidationError):\n+    \"\"\"Raised when a node registry is inconsistent.\"\"\"\n+\n+\n+class NodeRegistry:\n+    \"\"\"Small in-memory registry keyed by node id.\"\"\"\n+\n+    def __init__(self, nodes: Iterable[NodeDefinition | dict[str, Any]] = ()) -> None:\n+        self._nodes: dict[str, NodeDefinition] = {}\n+        for node in nodes:\n+            self.register(node)\n+\n+    def register(self, node: NodeDefinition | dict[str, Any]) -> NodeDefinition:\n+        definition = validate_node_definition(node)\n+        if definition.id in self._nodes:\n+            raise NodeRegistryError(f\"duplicate node id {definition.id!r}\")\n+        self._nodes[definition.id] = definition\n+        return definition\n+\n+    def get(self, node_id: str) -> NodeDefinition:\n+        try:\n+            return self._nodes[node_id]\n+        except KeyError as exc:\n+            raise KeyError(f\"unknown node id {node_id!r}\") from exc\n+\n+    def list(self, kind: str | None = None) -> tuple[NodeDefinition, ...]:\n+        if kind is not None and kind not in {\"built_in\", \"external\"}:\n+            raise NodeRegistryError(\"kind must be one of ['built_in', 'external']\")\n+        nodes = self._nodes.values()\n+        if kind is not None:\n+            nodes = [node for node in nodes if node.kind == kind]\n+        return tuple(sorted(nodes, key=lambda node: node.id))\n+\n+    def validate_all(self) -> tuple[NodeDefinition, ...]:\n+        for node in self._nodes.values():\n+            validate_node_definition(node)\n+        self._validate_graph_references()\n+        return self.list()\n+\n+    def to_dict(self, kind: str | None = None) -> dict[str, Any]:\n+        return {\"nodes\": [node.to_dict() for node in self.list(kind=kind)]}\n+\n+    def to_json(self, *, kind: str | None = None, indent: int | None = 2) -> str:\n+        return json.dumps(self.to_dict(kind=kind), indent=indent, sort_keys=True)\n+\n+    def as_mapping(self) -> MappingProxyType[str, NodeDefinition]:\n+        return MappingProxyType(dict(self._nodes))\n+\n+    def _validate_graph_references(self) -> None:\n+        known_ids = set(self._nodes)\n+        for node in self._nodes.values():\n+            for dependency in node.graph.depends_on:\n+                if dependency not in known_ids:\n+                    raise NodeRegistryError(f\"node {node.id!r} depends on unknown node {dependency!r}\")\n+                if dependency == node.id:\n+                    raise NodeRegistryError(f\"node {node.id!r} cannot depend on itself\")\n+\n+\n+def load_builtin_nodes() -> tuple[NodeDefinition, ...]:\n+    return builtin_nodes()\n+\n+\n+def load_curated_nodes() -> tuple[NodeDefinition, ...]:\n+    nodes:         Idea:
Implement the evolved ArtAgents custom node architecture discussed with the user. Treat every executable ArtAgents pipeline stage as a built-in node with typed input/output metadata, while supporting curated installable/wrapped external nodes such as Moirae. Add a node registry, schema, runner/adapter layer, and CLI exposure through the main pipeline tool so users can list, inspect, validate, and run nodes. Preserve legacy pipeline behavior. Use Moirae (https://github.com/peteromallet/Moirae) as the external wrapped-node boundary test in planning and, if feasible, include a local curated node manifest or fixture for it. Design should support optional inputs, output modes including mutate/create_or_replace, cache policy, conditions/graphs, and subprocess isolation. Implement a pragmatic MVP with tests that prove built-in node discovery and at least one node execution/validation path work without requiring expensive APIs or external binaries. Avoid overwriting unrelated dirty worktree changes.

User notes and answers:
- Steer implementation toward the simplest practical MVP. Avoid unnecessary abstraction, broad graph machinery, or premature marketplace/install complexity. Prefer a small node schema, registry, CLI list/inspect/validate/run, wrappers around existing scripts, and focused tests. Preserve legacy behavior. Use GPT-5.5 high instead of GPT-5.4 where possible for this orchestration.

        Idea:
Implement the evolved ArtAgents custom node architecture discussed with the user. Treat every executable ArtAgents pipeline stage as a built-in node with typed input/output metadata, while supporting curated installable/wrapped external nodes such as Moirae. Add a node registry, schema, runner/adapter layer, and CLI exposure through the main pipeline tool so users can list, inspect, validate, and run nodes. Preserve legacy pipeline behavior. Use Moirae (https://github.com/peteromallet/Moirae) as the external wrapped-node boundary test in planning and, if feasible, include a local curated node manifest or fixture for it. Design should support optional inputs, output modes including mutate/create_or_replace, cache policy, conditions/graphs, and subprocess isolation. Implement a pragmatic MVP with tests that prove built-in node discovery and at least one node execution/validation path work without requiring expensive APIs or external binaries. Avoid overwriting unrelated dirty worktree changes.

User notes and answers:
- Steer implementation toward the simplest practical MVP. Avoid unnecessary abstraction, broad graph machinery, or premature marketplace/install complexity. Prefer a small node schema, registry, CLI list/inspect/validate/run, wrappers around existing scripts, and focused tests. Preserve legacy behavior. Use GPT-5.5 high instead of GPT-5.4 where possible for this orchestration.

        Idea:
Implement the evolved ArtAgents custom node architecture discussed with the user. Treat every executable ArtAgents pipeline stage as a built-in node with typed input/output metadata, while supporting curated installable/wrapped external nodes such as Moirae. Add a node registry, schema, runner/adapter layer, and CLI exposure through the main pipeline tool so users can list, inspect, validate, and run nodes. Preserve legacy pipeline behavior. Use Moirae (https://github.com/peteromallet/Moirae) as the external wrapped-node boundary test in planning and, if feasible, include a local curated node manifest or fixture for it. Design should support optional inputs, output modes including mutate/create_or_replace, cache policy, conditions/graphs, and subprocess isolation. Implement a pragmatic MVP with tests that prove built-in node discovery and at least one node execution/validation path work without requiring expensive APIs or external binaries. Avoid overwriting unrelated dirty worktree changes.

User notes and answers:
- Steer implementation toward the simplest practical MVP. Avoid unnecessary abstraction, broad graph machinery, or premature marketplace/install complexity. Prefer a small node schema, registry, CLI list/inspect/validate/run, wrappers around existing scripts, and focused tests. Preserve legacy behavior. Use GPT-5.5 high instead of GPT-5.4 where possible for this orchestration.

self.invoke(root, [\"--render\"])\n*** Update File: README.md\n@@\n Each CLI still reads and writes plain files on disk so you can inspect intermediate JSON, run stages independently, and rerun only the step you need. The implementation lives in the `artagents/` package; root-level `*.py` files are compatibility launchers for existing `python3 pipeline.py ...` and `python3 cut.py ...` workflows. The top-level orchestrator is `pipeline.py --video SRC --brief BRIEF --out DIR`, which builds source-level artifacts in `DIR/` and brief-level artifacts in `DIR/briefs/<slug>/`.\n \n+## Nodes\n+\n+`pipeline.py nodes ...` exposes the executable stages as typed node metadata without replacing the legacy pipeline runner. Built-in nodes wrap the existing `pipeline.py` steps, and curated external nodes are metadata-only manifests.\n+\n+```bash\n+python3 pipeline.py nodes list\n+python3 pipeline.py nodes list --json --kind external\n+python3 pipeline.py nodes inspect builtin.render --json\n+python3 pipeline.py nodes validate\n+python3 pipeline.py nodes run builtin.render --out runs/example --brief brief.txt --dry-run\n+```\n+\n+The MVP includes `external.moirae` as a curated manifest for Moirae. It does not install Moirae or its tools; real execution requires a local Moirae install plus its declared binaries. Validation only checks those binaries when `--check-binaries` is passed:\n+\n+```bash\n+python3 pipeline.py nodes validate external.moirae --check-binaries\n+python3 pipeline.py nodes run external.moirae --out runs/moirae --input screenplay=script.md --dry-run\n+```\n+\n+Curated manifest discovery is verified from this source checkout under `artagents/nodes/curated/`. If packaging metadata is added later, include `artagents/nodes/curated/*.json` as package data so installed distributions can discover the same manifests.\n+\n ## Pure-generative and no-audio modes\n \n `pipeline.py` also supports an audio-backed no-video path:\n*** End Patch\n"
}
```
